                           RecSpecies_L@1000000004 : Record 8052988;
                           CMCSpeciesSetup_L@1000000000 : Record 8053323;
                         BEGIN
                           CurrReport.LANGUAGE := Language.GetLanguageID("Language Code");
                           LineCount_G := 0;
                           CompanyInfo.GET;
                           CompanyInfo.CALCFIELDS(CompanyInfo.Picture,CompanyInfo."Certificate Logo");

                           IF RespCenter.GET("Responsibility Center") THEN BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.RespCenter(CompanyAddr,RespCenter);
                             CompanyInfo."Phone No." := RespCenter."Phone No.";
                             CompanyInfo."Fax No." := RespCenter."Fax No.";
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             FormatAddr.Company(CompanyAddr,CompanyInfo);
                           END;

                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Inv. Header");
                           PostedDocDim1.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");

                           IF "Order No." = '' THEN
                             OrderNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             OrderNoText := FIELDCAPTION("Order No.");
                           IF "Purchaser Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             CLEAR(SalesPurchPerson);
                             PurchaserText := '';
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             SalesPurchPerson.GET("Purchaser Code");
                             PurchaserText := Text000
                           END;
                           IF "Your Reference" = '' THEN
                             ReferenceText := ''
                           ELSE
                             ReferenceText := FIELDCAPTION("Your Reference");
                           IF "VAT Registration No." = '' THEN
                             VATNoText := ''
                           ELSE
                             VATNoText := FIELDCAPTION("VAT Registration No.");
                           IF "Currency Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                             GLSetup.TESTFIELD("LCY Code");
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text006,GLSetup."LCY Code");
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             TotalText := STRSUBSTNO(Text001,"Currency Code");
                             TotalInclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text002,"Currency Code");
                             TotalExclVATText := STRSUBSTNO(Text006,"Currency Code");
                           END;
                           FormatAddr.PurchInvPayTo(VendAddr,"Purch. Inv. Header");
                           IF "Payment Terms Code" = '' THEN
                             PaymentTerms.INIT
                           ELSE BEGIN
                             PaymentTerms.GET("Payment Terms Code");
                             PaymentTerms.TranslateDescription(PaymentTerms,"Language Code");
                           END;

                           Pmethod_G := '';
                           IF "Purch. Inv. Header"."Payment Method Code"='' THEN
                            PaymentMethod_G.INIT
                           ELSE
                            BEGIN
                             PaymentMethod_G.GET("Payment Method Code");
                             Pmethod_G := PaymentMethod_G.Description;
                             Vend_G.GET("Purch. Inv. Header"."Buy-from Vendor No.");
                             IF Vend_G."Default Bank Account Code"<>'' THEN
                              BEGIN
                               DefVendBkAccount_G.GET(Vend_G."No.",Vend_G."Default Bank Account Code");
                               Pmethod_G +=' '+DefVendBkAccount_G."Bank Branch No."+' '+DefVendBkAccount_G."Agency Code"+

                              END;
                            END;

                           IF "Shipment Method Code" = '' THEN

                           ELSE BEGIN
                             ShipmentMethod.GET("Shipment Method Code");
                             ShipmentMethod.TranslateDescription(ShipmentMethod,"Language Code");
                           END;

                           FormatAddr.PurchInvShipTo(ShipToAddr,"Purch. Inv. Header");

                           IF LogInteraction THEN
                             IF NOT CurrReport.PREVIEW THEN
                               SegManagement.LogDocument(
                                 14,"No.",0,0,DATABASE::Vendor,"Buy-from Vendor No.","Purchaser Code",'',"Posting Description",'');

                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN
                             PricesInclVATtxt := FORMAT("Purch. Inv. Header"."Prices Including VAT");

                           PurchInvLine_L.RESET;
                           PurchInvLine_L.SETFILTER(PurchInvLine_L."Document No.","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                           PurchInvLine_L.SETFILTER(PurchInvLine_L."Collection recept No.",'<>%1','');
                           PurchInvLine_L.SETFILTER("Collection Recept Line",'<>0');
                           IF PurchInvLine_L.FINDFIRST THEN BEGIN
                             // --> 20140415-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1983-OAR01
                             // BEFORE

                             IF "Collection Invoice Type" <> "Collection Invoice Type"::Supplement THEN

                           G_recLivBord.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                           // --> 20130404-Cer15-CMC02.02.00-DRN01

                           //AFTER

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop1;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Inv. Header;
      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }
       }
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table123;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=VAR
                        PurchInvLine@1000 : Record 123;
                        VATIdentifier@1001 : Code[10];
                      BEGIN
                        VATAmountLine.DELETEALL;
                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount","Inv. Discount Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT");


                        IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                          PurchInvLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Inv. Header"."No.");
                          PurchInvLine.SETFILTER(Type,'<>%1',0);
                          VATAmountText :='';
                          IF PurchInvLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
                            VATAmountText := STRSUBSTNO(Text011,PurchInvLine."VAT %");
                            VATIdentifier := PurchInvLine."VAT Identifier";
                            REPEAT
                              IF (PurchInvLine."VAT Identifier" <> VATIdentifier) AND (PurchInvLine.Quantity <> 0) THEN
                                VATAmountText := Text012;
                            UNTIL PurchInvLine.NEXT = 0;
                          END;
                        END;

                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(G_MttLine);
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           L_i@1000000003 : Integer;
                           RecAverageReceiptAnalysis_L@1000000002 : Record 8053105;
                           L_recPurchLineVariatPrice@1000000001 : Record 8053005;
                           AnalyseCharacteristic_L@1000000000 : Record 8053092;
                         BEGIN
                           // --> 20123110-Rdl01-CMC02.01.00-WSA01
                           CASE "Purch. Inv. Line".Type OF
                                "Purch. Inv. Line".Type::" ":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=0;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line".Type::"G/L Account":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=1;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line".Type::Item:  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=2;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line".Type::"3":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=3;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line".Type::"Fixed Asset":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=4;
                           END;

                           CASE "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type" OF
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::" ":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=0;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Collection:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=1;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Shipping:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=2;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Disinsection:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=3;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::"Fixe Storage":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=4;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::"Variable Storage":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=5;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Administrative:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=6;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Drying:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=7;
                                "Purch. Inv. Line"."Line Collection Type"::Tax:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=8;

                           END;
                           IF ISSERVICETIER THEN

                             IF ((Type = 0) AND (Description <> '')) THEN
                               LineCount_G += 1;

                           // --> 20130404-Cer15-CMC02.02.00-DRN01
                           //BEFORE

                             ("Purch. Inv. Line".Type="Purch. Inv. Line".Type::"G/L Account")

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Inv. Line");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Document No.");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Inv. Line"."Line No.");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim2.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim2.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

      }
      SECTIONS
      {
      }
       }
    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounter;

                        IF VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0 THEN

                          VATAmountLine."Invoice Discount Amount",VATAmountLine."VAT Base",VATAmountLine."VAT Amount",
                          VATAmountLine."Amount Including VAT");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounterLCY;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (NOT GLSetup."Print VAT specification in LCY") OR
                           ("Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Code"  = '') OR
                           (VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0) THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,VATAmountLine.COUNT);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(VALVATBaseLCY,VALVATAmountLCY);

                        IF GLSetup."LCY Code" = '' THEN
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + Text008
                        ELSE
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text007 + FORMAT(GLSetup."LCY Code");

                        CurrExchRate.FindCurrency("Purch. Inv. Header"."Posting Date","Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Code",1);
                        CalculatedExchRate := ROUND(1 / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor" * CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount",0.000001);
                        VALExchRate := STRSUBSTNO(Text009,CalculatedExchRate,CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);

                           VALVATBaseLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Base" / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor");
                           VALVATAmountLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Amount" / "Purch. Inv. Header"."Currency Factor");
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table123;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.)
                          WHERE(Line Collection Type=CONST(Collection));
        DataItemVarName=ReceptLine;
        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           L_i@1000000003 : Integer;
                         BEGIN
                           // --> 20140415-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1983-OAR01
                           // BEFORE
                           // IF NOT recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Collection recept No.",
                           //    "Collection Recept Line") THEN
                           //   CurrReport.SKIP;
                           // AFTER
                           IF "Purch. Inv. Header"."Collection Invoice Type" <> "Purch. Inv. Header"."Collection Invoice Type"::Supplement THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                                            "Collection recept No.","Collection Recept Line") THEN
                               CurrReport.SKIP;
                           END ELSE BEGIN
                             recPostReceipLinePrestations_G.GET(recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                                                "Collection recept No.","Collection Recept Line");
                             recPostReceipLine_G.GET(recPostReceipLine_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                                     "Collection recept No.",recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receips Line No. Link");
                           END;
                           // <-- 20140415-Wi00-CMC02.02.00-1983-OAR01

                           IF NOT CollectReceipts_G.GET(CollectReceipts_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,ReceptLine."Collection recept No.") THEN
                            CollectReceipts_G.INIT;


                           //R'sultats d'analyse
                           CLEAR(G_ResulAnalyse);
                           G_recPostAnalyse.RESET;
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETCURRENTKEY(G_recPostAnalyse."Sort Order");
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt Type",G_recPostAnalyse."Receipt Type"::Receipt);
                           G_recPostAnalyse.SETRANGE("Receipt No.","Collection recept No.");
                           IF G_recPostAnalyse.FINDSET THEN BEGIN
                             L_i:=1;
                             REPEAT
                               G_ResulAnalyse[L_i]:=G_recPostAnalyse.Value;
                               L_i:=L_i+1;
                             UNTIL G_recPostAnalyse.NEXT=0;
                           END;
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Inv. Header;
        DataItemLink=Document No.=FIELD(No.);
      }

      IF "Purch. Inv. Header"."Prepayment Invoice" THEN
        EXIT(Text010);
      EXIT(Text004);
    END;

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object

Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
End If
End Function

Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
  If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data1 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data2 = NewData
  End If

  If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
      Data3 = NewData
  End If
End Function

Shared PictureData1 as Object
Shared PictureData2 as Object
Shared PictureData3 as Object

Public Function GetPicture(Group as integer) as Object
if Group = 1 then
   Return PictureData1
End If

if Group = 2 then
   Return PictureData2
End If

if Group = 3 then
   Return PictureData3
End If
End Function
